 QEMU40LE — 5D4 MPU MISSING PHASES AUDIT
============================================================

===== 5D4 TABLE =====
     1	 /**
     2	 * Minimal generic spell set based on 60D (deleted everything that did not prevent booting)
     3	 * 
     4	 * Without card support (comment out PROP_CARD2_EXISTS), it boots most EOS models (menu navigation works)
     5	 * Tested on: 5D2 5D3 6D 50D 60D 70D 450D 500D 550D 600D 650D 700D 100D 1000D 1100D 1200D 1300D EOSM2.
     6	 * Not working: EOSM.
     7	 * Does something: 80D, 750D, 760D.
     8	 *
     9	 * With SD card support (unmodified - PROP_CARD2_EXISTS enabled), it initializes SD
    10	 * on all the above EOS models, but may crash at EstimatedSize on the most recent ones.
    11	 * Even if it crashes, the SD card is initialized and the firmware can save files to the virtual card.
    12	 * Working (menu navigation, card format): 60D 500D 550D 600D 650D 700D 1100D 1200D 1300D.
    13	 * Working (menu navigation, card recognized): 450D 1000D.
    14	 * Assert at EstimatedSize, dumpf works: 5D3 6D 70D 80D 100D EOSM2.
    15	 * Not working EOSM.
    16	 * 
    17	 * With CF card support (use PROP_CARD1_EXISTS, 0x21 -> 0x20):
    18	 * Working: 50D 5D2.
    19	 * Not working: 5D3.
    20	 * 
    21	 */
    22	
    23	
    24	static struct mpu_init_spell mpu_init_spells_5D4[] = {
    25	    { { 0x06, 0x04, 0x02, 0x00, 0x00 }, .description = "Init", .out_spells = { /* spell #1 */
    26	        { 0x2c, 0x2a, 0x02, 0x00, 0x03, 0x03, 0x03, 0x04, 0x03, 0x00, 0x00, 0x48, 0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x14, 0x50, 0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x81, 0x06, 0x00, 0x00, 0x04, 0x06, 0x00, 0x00, 0x04, 0x06, 0x00, 0x00, 0x04, 0x01, 0x01, 0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x4d, 0x4b, 0x01 },/* reply #1.17, Init */
    27	        { 0x06, 0x05, 0x01, 0x21, 0x01, 0x00 },                 /* reply #1.3, PROP_CARD2_EXISTS */
    28	
    29	        /*
    30	         * EOS 5D Mark IV FW 1.3.3 PROP_VIDEO_MODE.
    31	         *
    32	         * 5D4 uses the modern 0x26/0x24 MPU frame.
    33	         * 0x09C4 was observed from Canon FW and is required
    34	         * during startup before EstimatedSize initialization.
    35	         */
    36	        {
    37	            0x26, 0x24, 0x01, 0x4e,
    38	            0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x00,
    39	            0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x00,
    40	            0x00, 0x00, 0x09, 0xc4,
    41	            0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x01,
    42	            0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x58,
    43	            0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x01,
    44	            0x00, 0x00, 0x00, 0x55,
    45	            0x80, 0x00, 0x00, 0x3f,
    46	            0x00, 0x00
    47	        }, /* PROP_VIDEO_MODE */
    48	
    49	        { 0 } } },
    50
